This provision, therefore, may require edits at the final rule stage, to accurately align both rules.
美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)认识到政策和法规变化会影响人员配备需求、成本、费用收入和处理时间。CBP将考虑这些其他规则制定活动对本规则的外围性、重叠性或相互关联性影响,并将在日后分析并纳入可能影响本提案或与本提案存在实质性重叠的规则(如有)的潜在影响。
CBP realizes that policy and regulatory changes can affect staffing needs, costs, fee revenue, and processing times. CBP will consider these other rulemakings for peripheral, overlapping, or interrelated effects on this rule, and will analyze and incorporate the potential effects of rules that may impact or substantively overlap with this proposal, if any, at a later date.
六、法定和法规审查
VI. Statutory and Regulatory Reviews
A. 第12866号、第13563号和第14192号行政命令
A. Executive Orders 12866, 13563, and 14192
第12866号行政命令(《法规规划和审查》)和第13563号行政命令(《改善法规和法规审查》)指示各机构评估现有监管方案的成本和收益,如果确有必要进行监管,则应选择能使净收益最大化的监管方式。第13563号行政命令强调量化成本和收益、降低成本、协调规则以及促进灵活性的重要性。第14192号行政命令 (印刷页第64850页) (《通过放松监管释放繁荣》)指示各机构大幅降低遵守联邦法规所需的私人支出,并规定“在法律允许的范围内,与新法规相关的任何新增增量成本,应通过取消与至少10项现行法规相关的现有成本来抵消。”
Executive Orders 12866 (Regulatory Planning and Review) and 13563 (Improving Regulation and Regulatory Review) direct agencies to assess the costs and benefits of available regulatory alternatives and, if regulation is necessary, to select regulatory approaches that maximize net benefits. Executive Order 13563 emphasizes the importance of quantifying both costs and benefits, of reducing costs, of harmonizing rules, and of promoting flexibility. Executive Order 14192 ( printed page 64850) (Unleashing Prosperity Through Deregulation) directs agencies to significantly reduce the private expenditures required to comply with Federal regulations and provides that “any new incremental costs associated with new regulations shall, to the extent permitted by law, be offset by the elimination of existing costs associated with at least 10 prior regulations.”
管理和预算办公室(OMB)已根据第12866号行政命令第3(f)节将本拟议规则指定为“重大监管行动”,但根据第3(f)(1)节,该规则并非具有经济意义的重大规则。因此,该规则已经OMB审查。
The Office of Management and Budget (OMB) has designated this proposed rule a “significant regulatory action,” under section 3(f) of Executive Order 12866, although not economically significant under section 3(f)(1). Accordingly, the rule has been reviewed by OMB.
关于本拟议规则根据第14192号行政命令要求是否被视为“监管性”或“放松监管性”的确定,将在本规则制定程序中发布任何最终规则时作出。 参见 OMB备忘录《落实第14192号行政命令第3节的指南》,题为《通过放松监管释放繁荣》(2025年3月26日)。
A determination of whether this proposed rule is considered “regulatory” or “deregulatory” under the requirements of Executive Order 14192 will be made when any final rule in this rulemaking is issued. See OMB's Memorandum “Guidance Implementing Section 3 of Executive Order 14192,” titled `Unleashing Prosperity Through Deregulation' (March 26, 2025).
1. 本规则的目的
1. Purpose of the Rule
在实施自愿报告之前,国际铁路承运人在列车抵达美国或从美国口岸(POE)出境之前,不向CBP传输车上旅客的信息。[27] 因此,有关列车乘客和机组人员的信息未能及时提供给CBP,无法充分准确地评估计划通过铁路跨越美国边境的个人所构成的潜在威胁。CBP确实收到了载有商业货物的列车抵达或离开美国的预计到达时间。该信息是以非正式和自愿的方式提供的。
Before the implementation of voluntary reporting, international rail carriers did not transmit information to CBP for travelers on board a train prior to arriving in or departing from the United States at a port of entry (POE).[27] As a result, information regarding train passengers and crew was not provided to CBP in time to adequately and accurately assess potential threats posed by individuals planning to cross the U.S. border by train. CBP did receive an estimated time of arrival for trains arriving in or departing from the United States and carrying commercial cargo. This information was provided informally and voluntarily.
这种信息缺失造成了国家安全方面的漏洞,恐怖分子和其他罪犯可能利用该漏洞跨越美国边境或在不被发现的情况下运输武器和违禁品。[28] 对于铁路旅行而言,这一风险更为突出,因为其他旅行方式,例如航空和海运,通常都要求提供预先旅客信息,CBP利用这些信息识别恐怖分子和其他罪犯构成的潜在威胁。
This lack of information resulted in a gap in national security that terrorists and other criminals could exploit to cross the U.S. border or transport weapons and contraband without detection.[28] This risk is increased for rail travel because other modes of travel, such as by air and sea, generally require advance traveler information, which CBP uses to identify potential threats from terrorists and other criminals.
为缓解这一安全漏洞,CBP于2005年在铁路领域建立了一项自愿报告计划,使铁路承运人能够为跨越美国边境的列车传输预先旅客信息。自2005年以来,已有三家客运铁路承运人通过该自愿计划向CBP传输预先旅客信息,但目前仅有一家铁路承运人参与该自愿计划。[29] 没有货运铁路承运人参与该自愿计划。CBP已能够利用自愿传输的数据,在列车抵达边境之前识别需要接受额外审查或检查的旅客,并识别违反移民、海关、农业和其他法律的行为,同时通过节省乘客和承运人在边境等候的时间,便利合法旅客的国际旅行。然而,由于传输预先旅客信息并非强制性要求,并非所有铁路承运人都传输此信息,且其传输的信息也并不总是有效、准确或及时。因此,该安全漏洞依然存在。
To mitigate this security gap, in 2005, CBP established a voluntary reporting program in the rail environment that enables rail carriers to transmit advance traveler information for trains crossing the U.S. border. Since 2005, three passenger rail carriers transmitted advance traveler information to CBP through the voluntary program; however, only one rail carrier currently participates in the voluntary program.[29] No freight rail carriers have participated in the voluntary program. CBP has been able to use the voluntarily transmitted data to identify travelers who require additional vetting or inspection and to identify violations of immigration, customs, agriculture, and other laws prior to a train's arrival at the border while simultaneously facilitating the international travel of legitimate travelers by saving passengers and carriers time waiting at the border. However, because transmission of advance traveler information is not mandatory, not all rail carriers transmit this information and the information they do transmit is not always valid, accurate, or timely. Accordingly, the security gap is still present.
本拟议规则将通过要求所有抵达或离开美国的列车强制传输预先旅客信息以及抵达或离境通知,进一步缓解国家安全风险。
This proposed rule would further mitigate the risk to national security by making the transmission of advance traveler information and a notice of arrival or departure mandatory for all trains arriving in or departing from the United States.
2. 本规则的成本和收益
2. Costs and Benefits of Rule
在本监管影响分析中，美国海关与边境保护局(U.S. Customs and Border Protection，CBP)以定性方式，并在可能的情况下以定量和货币化方式，讨论国际铁路承运人、旅客及CBP在实施本规则后将经历的成本和收益。本拟议规则的分析期为2026年至2030年。此外，自2005年以来，铁路承运人、旅客及CBP已就自愿报告计划经历了成本和收益。虽然并非本规则的结果，CBP也将讨论自愿报告计划对铁路承运人和CBP的影响。自愿报告计划始于2005年，但有关铁路承运人的高质量CBP数据始于2012年。因此，自愿报告计划的分析期为2012年至2025年。本规则与自愿报告期的影响可以相加，以得出这两个分析期的总体影响。
In this regulatory impact analysis, CBP discusses the costs and benefits that international rail carriers, passengers, and CBP would experience with the implementation of this rule in qualitative, and when possible, quantitative, and monetized terms. The period of analysis for this proposed rule is from 2026 through 2030. Additionally, rail carriers, passengers, and CBP have experienced costs and benefits involving the voluntary reporting program since 2005. Though not a result of this rule, CBP will also discuss the effects of the voluntary reporting program for rail carriers and CBP. The voluntary reporting program began in 2005, but quality CBP data on rail carriers begins in 2012. Therefore, the period of the analysis of the voluntary reporting program spans from 2012 to 2025. The effects of this rule and the voluntary reporting period can be added together to see the total effect of these two periods of analysis.
a. 成本
a. Costs
本规则将为客运铁路承运人和货运铁路承运人带来新的成本，这主要是由于准备和传输所需信息需要额外的时间。由于客运铁路承运人和货运铁路承运人承受不同的时间负担，将分别讨论其成本。CBP因本规则而承担与人力及研究开发相关的成本。CBP未发现本规则将为旅客带来任何新增成本。CBP征求关于旅客因本规则而可能承担的任何潜在成本的意见，包括与旅客行为或出行模式变化相关的任何成本。
This rule would introduce new costs to both passenger rail carriers and cargo rail carriers largely as a result of additional time necessary to prepare, and transmit required information. The costs for passenger rail carriers and cargo rail carriers will be discussed separately as they experience different time burdens. CBP experiences costs related to labor and research and development as a result of this rule. CBP did not identify any new costs for passengers that would result from this rule. CBP requests comment on any potential costs, including any costs associated with changes in passenger behavior or travel patterns, that would be borne by passengers as a result of this rule.
i. 国际客运铁路承运人
i. International Passenger Rail Carriers
本规则将为国际客运铁路承运人带来成本，因为要求其在抵达边境前不迟于24小时传输到达/离境通知，并在列车从最后一站或停靠点出发前往美国边境之前，向CBP传输旅行者信息(包括旅客和机组人员信息)。[30] 在从途经边境的车站或停靠点出发前，如果在旅行者舱单传输给CBP后有人员上车或下车，铁路承运人还将被要求传输对已传输旅行者信息的修正。CBP征求关于国际客运铁路承运人因本规则而承担的任何其他成本或运营变化的意见。
This rule would introduce costs to international passenger rail carriers by requiring them to transmit a notice of arrival/departure no later than 24 hours before arrival at the border and traveler information (including both passenger and crew information) to CBP before the train departs from the final station or stop to approach the U.S. border.[30] Before departing a station or stop en route to the border, rail carriers would also be required to transmit amendments to the transmitted traveler information if a person boards or disembarks the train after the traveler manifest has been transmitted to CBP. CBP requests comment on any other costs or changes to operations that would be borne by international passenger rail carriers as a result of this rule.
国际客运铁路承运人有多种方式记录舱单所需的旅客数据。其中一种方式是铁路承运人使用其可能已经拥有或可免费获得的程序，将提前旅客信息系统(APIS)信息记录到电子表格中。为向CBP传输旅行者舱单，铁路承运人可利用免费的eAPIS网络门户。国际客运铁路承运人已经拥有计算机、互联网接入、用于记录舱单的软件，并可通过免费的eAPIS网络门户向CBP传输舱单。CBP还计划允许铁路承运人使用任何经CBP批准的具有舱单功能的应用程序或系统，直接从文件向CBP传输旅行者信息。CBP认为，本拟议规则仅会为国际客运铁路承运人带来最低限度的技术成本，因为它们已经拥有传输舱单所需的大部分资源，并可利用免费的eAPIS网络门户。 (印刷页第64851页)
International passenger rail carriers have several options for how they could record passenger data for the manifests. One option is for rail carriers to record APIS information into an electronic spreadsheet using programs that they are likely to already have access to or are available for free. To transmit the traveler manifest to CBP, rail carriers can take advantage of the free eAPIS web portal. International passenger rail carriers already have computers, internet access, software to record manifests, and may transmit manifests to CBP through the free eAPIS web portal. CBP also plans to allow rail carriers to use any CBP-approved application or system with manifest capabilities to transmit traveler information directly from documents to CBP. CBP believes that this proposed rule would require only minimal technology costs for international passenger rail carriers because they already have most resources needed to transmit a manifest and could take advantage of the free eAPIS web portal. ( printed page 64851)
国际客运铁路承运人将承担与准备和传输到达/离境通知、旅客舱单、机组人员舱单及舱单修正相关的成本。根据其在自愿报告计划方面的经验，CBP认为客运铁路承运人将单独传输机组人员舱单信息，并在传输旅客舱单信息之前进行传输。因此，为本分析之目的，CBP将收集和提交机组人员信息相关的成本与收集和提交旅客舱单相关的成本分开估算。
International passenger rail carriers would experience costs associated with the preparation and transmission of notices of arrival/departure, passenger manifests, crew manifests, and amendments to manifests. Based on its experience with the voluntary reporting program, CBP believes passenger rail carriers would transmit crew manifest information separately and prior to the transmission of passenger manifest information. Accordingly, for the purpose of this analysis, CBP estimated the costs associated with the collection and submission of crew information separately from the costs associated with the collection and submission of passenger manifests.
根据本规则,对于每一列打算跨越美国边境的列车,国际客运铁路承运人在所有情况下都将被要求传送一份到达/离境通知和一份到达/离境舱单。此外,如果舱单信息在传送给美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)后发生任何变化,国际客运铁路承运人将被要求提交经修订的舱单。根据CBP在自愿计划方面的经验,CBP现场行动办公室(OFO)估计,由于乘客在最终抵达边境前沿途车站或停靠点上下车,且此时旅客信息已经初次传送完毕,平均每列客运列车将传送五次修订。CBP通过将表1所示的客运列车数量乘以到达/离境通知数量(1份)、旅客舱单数量(1份)、机组人员舱单数量(1份)以及舱单修订数量(5次),估算出预期的传送总数。[31] 表2列出了每个日历年度预计的传送数量。
Under this rule, for each train intending to cross the U.S. border, international passenger rail carriers would be required to transmit one notice of arrival/departure and one arrival/departure manifest in all cases. Additionally, international passenger rail carriers would be required to submit amended manifests if any of the manifest information changes after it is transmitted to CBP. Based on CBP's experience with the voluntary program, CBP's Office of Field Operations (OFO) estimates that an average of five amendments would be transmitted per passenger train, due to passengers boarding or departing at stations or stops along the route before the final approach to the border and after the traveler information has been initially transmitted. CBP estimates the total transmissions expected by multiplying the number of passenger trains, shown in Table 1 by the number of notices of arrival/departure (1), passenger manifests (1), crew manifests (1), and manifest amendments (5).[31] The estimated number of transmissions per calendar year are shown in Table 2.

CBP主题专家估计,准备和传送一份预先到达/离境通知需要三分钟,一份旅客舱单需要十分钟,一份机组人员舱单需要三分钟,旅客舱单的一次修订需要三十秒。[32 33]
CBP subject matter experts estimate preparing and transmitting an advance notice of arrival/departure to take three minutes, a passenger manifest to take ten minutes, a crew manifest to take three minutes, and an amendment for a passenger manifest to take 30 seconds.[32 33]

国际客运铁路承运人在被要求就文件错误或CBP认定舱单上某人将不被允许进入美国的情况致电CBP时，也可能产生成本。CBP估计，这可能适用于所有抵达美国的铁路旅客中的百分之一，且该行业每年的总成本将低于1,000美元。由于该情形发生的罕见性及解决问题所需时间极短，CBP认为此项成本可忽略不计。CBP请求就此项估计提交意见。
International passenger rail carriers may also experience costs when required to call CBP related to document errors or CBP determining that someone on a manifest would not be allowed to enter the United States. CBP estimates that this could be required for one percent of all rail passengers arriving in the United States and that total costs to the industry would be less than $1,000 annually. CBP believes this cost to be negligible due to the rarity of the occurrence and the minimal time it would take to address issues. CBP requests comments on this estimate.
二、国际货运铁路承运人
ii. International Cargo Rail Carriers
与国际客运铁路承运人一样，国际货运铁路承运人可使用其很可能已经拥有或可免费获取的程序，将《乘客信息系统》（APIS）舱单信息记录到电子表格中。为向CBP传输APIS舱单，铁路承运人可利用eAPIS网络门户，铁路承运人可在该门户上创建免费账户并上传现有的数据表格。国际货运铁路承运人已拥有计算机、互联网接入、记录舱单所需的软件，并可通过免费的eAPIS网络门户向CBP传输舱单。CBP还计划允许铁路承运人使用任何经CBP批准、具备舱单处理能力的应用程序或系统，直接将旅客信息从文件传输至CBP。 （印刷页第64854页）
International cargo rail carriers, like international passenger rail carriers, can record APIS manifest information into an electronic spreadsheet using programs that they are likely to already have access to or that are available for free. To transmit the APIS manifest to CBP, rail carriers can take advantage of the eAPIS web portal to which a rail carrier can create a free account and upload an existing spreadsheet of data. International cargo rail carriers already have computers, internet access, software to record manifests, and may transmit manifests to CBP through the free eAPIS web portal. CBP also plans to allow rail carriers to use any CBP-approved application or system with manifest capabilities to transmit traveler information directly from documents to CBP. ( printed page 64854)
CBP认为,该拟议规则仅会使国际货运铁路承运人产生极小的技术成本,因为它们已经拥有传输舱单所需的大部分资源,并且可以利用免费的eAPIS网络门户。国际货运铁路承运人将因编制和传输所有在船旅客的到达/离境通知以及到达和离境舱单而产生成本。预计每种传输的总次数等于表1中所示的货运列车数量。CBP估计,2026年至2030年期间,每年的传输次数为70,215次。如前所述,CBP估计编制和传输一份到达/离境通知需要三分钟,编制和传输一份机组人员舱单需要三分钟。[35] CBP将3分钟(即0.05小时)的各自时间负担乘以传输次数,以估算总年度时间负担。CBP预计货运铁路承运人无需提交舱单修正,因为它们通常仅有少数几名机组人员在船,且没有乘客。由于没有货运铁路承运人参与自愿报告计划,2012年至2025年期间的年度时间负担和货币化成本为零。表7列出了2026年至2030年期间预计的总时间负担。
CBP believes that this proposed rule would require only minimal technology costs for international cargo rail carriers because they already have most resources needed to transmit a manifest and could take advantage of the free eAPIS web portal. International cargo rail carriers would experience costs associated with the preparation and transmission of notices of arrival/departure and arrival and departure manifests for all travelers on board. The total transmissions expected for each are assumed to be equal to the number of cargo trains shown in Table 1. For the years 2026-2030, CBP estimates the number of transmissions to be 70,215 annually. As previously stated, CBP estimates preparing and transmitting a notice of arrival/departure to take three minutes and a crew manifest to take three minutes.[35] CBP multiplied the respective time burdens of 3 minutes, or 0.05 hours, by the number of transmissions results to estimate the total annual time burden. CBP does not anticipate that cargo rail carriers will need to submit manifest amendments because they typically only have a couple crew members on-board and no passengers. Since no cargo rail carriers participated in the voluntary reporting program, the annual time and monetized cost for 2012-2025 are zero. The total expected time burden for years 2026-2030 are shown in Table 7.

随后,CBP将表7中的年度时间负担乘以机车工程师的估计工资率(每小时54.19美元),从而得出国际货运铁路承运人的估计年度成本,如表8所示。

iii. CBP
iii. CBP
由于本规则,CBP产生了与人工及研发相关的成本。为适应铁路承运人传输信息而进行的系统研发,使CBP在2012年产生了2,039,255美元的初始成本,此后四年每年产生611,766美元的成本,五年研发总成本为4,486,319美元。CBP预计因本规则而不会产生额外的研发成本。
CBP experiences costs related to labor and research and development as a result of this rule. Research and development of systems required to accommodate the transmission of information by rail carriers resulted in an initial cost to CBP of $2,039,255 in 2012 and annual costs of $611,766 for four years after for a five-year total cost of $4,486,319 for research and development. CBP does not expect additional research and development costs as a result of the rule.
在自愿报告计划期间,CBP主题专家报告称,对于每一列在抵达边境前已传输在船旅客舱单信息的客运列车,CBP官员平均需花费约十分钟进行初步审查并批准该列车驶近边境。[36] 这是因实施自愿报告计划而产生的新时间负担,并将作为本规则的一部分持续存在。该时间负担通过在边境节省的时间得以抵消,相关内容将在后文分析中论述。CBP通过将每列客运列车的时间负担(十分钟)乘以每年提前传输舱单后抵达或离境的客运列车数量(表9),估算出因自愿报告计划及本规则而产生的初步审查年度时间负担。 ( 印刷页 64855)
During the voluntary reporting program, CBP subject matter experts report that a CBP officer took approximately ten minutes on average to perform initial vetting and approve a train to approach the border for each passenger train which had transmitted manifest information for travelers on board the train prior to arrival at the border.[36] This is a new time burden resulting from the implementation of the voluntary reporting program and will continue as part of this rule. This time burden is offset by time saved at the border which is addressed later in the analysis. CBP estimated the annual ( printed page 64855) time burden of initial vetting as a result of the voluntary reporting program and this rule by multiplying the time burden per passenger train (ten minutes) by the annual number of passenger trains arriving or departing with a manifest transmitted in advance (Table 9).
CBP(美国海关与边境保护局)估计,对一列货运列车执行初步审查并批准其靠近边境所需的平均时间负担为五分钟。[37] CBP通过将每列货运列车的时间负担(五分钟)乘以每年提前传送舱单的货运列车抵达或离境数量(见表10),估算出货运列车初步审查的年度时间负担。在自愿申报项目中,没有货运列车承运人传送机组人员舱单,但根据本规则,所有货运列车承运人将被要求传送车上所有旅客的舱单,包括全体机组人员。CBP对客运列车和货运列车进行初步审查的总时间负担见表11。
CBP estimates the time burden to perform an initial vetting and approve a cargo train to approach the border to be on average five minutes.[37] CBP estimated the annual time burden of initial vetting for cargo trains by multiplying the time burden per cargo train (five minutes) by the annual number of cargo trains arriving or departing with a manifest transmitted in advance (Table 10). No cargo train carriers transmitted crew manifests as part of the voluntary reporting program but would be required to transmit manifests for all travelers on board, including all crew, as a result of this rule. CBP's total time burden for performing initial vetting for both passenger trains and cargo trains is shown in Table 11.

四、成本摘要
iv. Summary of Costs
利用表5中的数据,CBP(美国海关与边境保护局)估计,在2012年至2025年的自愿报告计划期间,客运铁路承运人平均每年产生未贴现成本25,946美元,预计在2026-2030年期间,由于本规则,平均每年将产生未贴现成本58,430美元,与无规则或无自愿报告计划的基准情形相比。[39] 利用表8中的数据,预计货运铁路承运人将因本规则而产生平均每年未贴现成本380,495美元。货运铁路承运人在自愿报告期间未产生任何成本,因为他们未参与该计划。CBP不预期乘客会因本规则而产生任何成本,因为CBP假设本规则要求的信息已经由乘客提供给CBP或铁路承运人。在自愿报告计划期间,CBP因初步审查而产生的平均每年未贴现成本为38,055美元。在2012年至2016年期间,CBP还为实施该计划的系统产生了总计4,486,319美元的研究与开发成本。CBP预计,由于本规则,与初步审查相关的平均每年成本将增加至未贴现的639,071美元,与无自愿报告计划的基准情形相比。CBP预计不会产生额外的研究与开发成本。
Using the data in Table 5, CBP estimates that passenger rail carriers experienced an average undiscounted annual cost of $25,946 during the voluntary reporting program between 2012 and 2025 and are expected to experience an average undiscounted annual cost of $58,430 in years 2026-2030 as a result of this rule compared to a baseline of no rule or voluntary reporting program.[39] Using data from Table 8, Cargo rail carriers are expected to experience an average undiscounted annual cost of $380,495 as a result of this rule. Cargo rail carriers experienced no costs as a result of the voluntary reporting period because they did not participate. CBP does not anticipate passengers to experience any costs as a result of this rule because CBP assumes the information required by this rule is already provided by passengers to either CBP or rail carriers. During the voluntary reporting program, CBP experienced an average undiscounted annual cost of $38,055 as a result of the initial vetting. Between 2012 and 2016, CBP also experienced total research and development costs of $4,486,319 for systems to implement this program. CBP expects the average annual cost attributed to initial vetting to increase to an undiscounted $639,071 as a result of this rule compared to a baseline of no voluntary reporting program. CBP does not expect additional research and development costs.
下表14显示了因本拟议规则预计对客运铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人和CBP产生的贴现成本。按3%和7%的贴现率计算,客运铁路承运人在2026年至2030年期间预计将分别产生总成本259,798美元和223,901美元,年化成本为58,430美元。从2026年到2030年,按3%和7%的贴现率计算,货运铁路承运人预计将分别产生总成本1,691,802美元和1,458,042美元,年化成本为380,495美元。按3%和7%的贴现率计算,CBP预计将分别产生总成本2,841,511美元和2,488,894美元,年化成本为639,071美元。
In Table 14 below, the discounted costs are shown for passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, and CBP, expected as a result of this proposed rule. Passenger rail carriers would be expected to experience a total cost from 2026 to 2030 of $259,798 and $223,901 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized costs of $58,430. From 2026 to 2030, cargo rail carriers would be expected to experience a total cost of $1,691,802 and $1,458,042 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized costs $380,495. CBP would be expected to experience a total cost of $2,841,511 and $2,488,894 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized costs of $639,071.

b. 收益
b. Benefits
当国际铁路承运人按照本拟议规则的要求,提前提供旅客的到达、离境及载客清单信息时,CBP将能够对经由火车跨越美国边境的个人进行初步核查,并允许进行额外的安全审查。这种额外的审查将显著减少客运铁路承运人及旅客在边境的等待时间,同时提高客运铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人及旅客的安全性。CBP也将从边境处的时间负担减少中受益。本部分对收益的讨论以不存在自愿申报项目或规则为基准情形。
When international rail carriers provide advance notice of arrival, departure, and manifest information for travelers, as would be required by this proposed rule, CBP would be able to perform initial vetting of the individuals crossing the U.S. border by train and allow for additional security vetting. This additional vetting would significantly reduce the wait times at the border for passenger rail carriers and passengers while increasing security for passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, and passengers. CBP would also benefit from the reduced time burden at the border. Benefits are discussed using a baseline of no voluntary reporting program or rule.
一、国际客运铁路承运人,机组人员
i. International Passenger Rail Carriers—Crew
本规则实施后,客运铁路承运人在边境等待CBP处理旅客的时间将有所减少。CBP现场业务办公室(OFO)估计,约有百分之一的离境客运列车在边境被扣留以接受检查;所有到达的客运列车均被扣留接受检查。[40] 根据CBP现场业务办公室(OFO)的数据,在抵达或离开美国之前未传输旅客或机组人员清单的客运列车,预计在入境口岸等待时间为一小时,期间CBP官员需对所有旅客的入境或出境进行处理。CBP预计,如本规则所述,提前传输机组人员及旅客清单信息,将使 (印刷页第64859页) 边境平均等待时间减少半小时。[41]
Upon implementation of this rule, passenger rail carriers would see a reduction in the time spent waiting at the border for CBP to process travelers. CBP OFO estimates that approximately one percent of departing passenger trains are held at the border for inspection; all arriving passenger trains are held for inspection.[40] According to CBP's Office of Field Operations (OFO), passenger trains that did not transmit a passenger or crew manifest before arriving in or departing from the United States could expect to have a one hour wait at the port of entry while CBP officers work to process all the travelers for entry or exit. CBP expects the advance transmission of crew and passenger manifest information, as described in this rule, would reduce the ( printed page 64859) wait at the border by half an hour on average.[41]
为估算客运列车机组人员的时间节省,CBP首先使用自愿项目中的清单数据,获取2012年至2025年期间客运列车上的机组人员数量,并发现该期间每列客运列车的平均机组人员数为4.46人。随后,CBP将4.46与2026年至2030年期间估计的客运铁路清单传输次数(表2)相乘,以估算这些年度的机组人员总数(表16)。
To estimate the time savings for passenger rail crew, CBP first used manifest data from the voluntary program to obtain the number of crew on a passenger train in years 2012-2025 and found the average number of crew in those years to be 4.46 per passenger train. CBP then multiplied 4.46 by the number of estimated passenger rail manifest transmissions (Table 2) in years 2026-2030, to estimate the total number of crew in those years (Table 16).

随后，CBP通过将节省的工时数（见表17）乘以估算的小时工资率，估算了客运铁路承运人机组人员的货币化总收益。CBP了解到，通常任何一列客运列车上都配有多个不同职位的员工，但由于缺乏关于其职位和工资率的信息 （印刷页第64860页） CBP使用机车工程师的工资率（每小时54.19美元）来估算这些员工为客运铁路承运人带来的收益。客运铁路承运人的货币化总收益见表18。
CBP then estimated the total monetized benefits for crew of passenger rail carriers by multiplying the person-hours saved (Table 17) by the estimated hourly wage rate. CBP understands that there are typically several employees of various roles on any passenger train but due to a lack of information regarding their positions ( printed page 64860) and wage rates, CBP uses the locomotive engineer wage rate ($54.19) for these employees to estimate the benefits for passenger rail carriers. The total monetized benefits for passenger rail carriers can be found in Table 18.

二、国际铁路旅客
ii. International Rail Passengers
由于本规则的实施，乘坐国际列车的旅客在美国边境等待的时间将会缩短，因为CBP可以在列车到达边境之前完成初步审查。为确定表20所示所有旅客节省的时间，CBP将每列客运列车节省的等待时间（0.5小时）乘以在美国边境经过处理且已向CBP提交载客清单的客运列车的旅客人数，具体见表19。[42] 随后，CBP将各类铁路旅客的小时工资率（70.45美元）乘以表20中节省的总时间，以货币化衡量本规则为旅客带来的节省，具体见表21。[43]
Passengers traveling internationally by rail would experience a shorter wait at the U.S. border as a result of this rule because CBP can perform the initial vetting before the train arrives at the border. To determine the time saved for all passengers shown in Table 20, CBP multiplied the wait time saved per passenger train (0.5 hours) by the number of passengers processed at the U.S. border on passenger trains for which a manifest has been transmitted to CBP, shown in Table 19.[42] CBP then multiplied the hourly wage rate for all-purpose rail travelers ($70.45) by the total time saved in Table 20 to monetize the savings passengers would experience as a result of this rule as shown in Table 21.[43]

三、国际货运铁路承运人
iii. International Cargo Rail Carriers
国际货运列车通常每天多次越境,且由同一班组人员随车。鉴于这一独特情况,CBP主题专家认为,国际货运铁路承运人将因本规则而获得一定的时间节省。目前,货运列车的乘务人员必须使列车完全停止,CBP才能在入境或出境时对乘务人员进行检查。随后乘务人员必须重新启动列车运行,这是一个耗时的过程。根据本规则,由于CBP将在列车抵达边境之前获得乘务人员信息,且很可能当天早些时候已对该乘务人员进行过处理,CBP可以在列车缓慢行驶时对乘务人员进行检查,而无需使列车完全停止。就此而言,若能为货运铁路承运人节省边境时间,则货运铁路承运人获得的收益将高于本分析中所报告的数值。CBP目前尚无关于此项时间节省的量化估计,并就此事项征求意见。
International cargo trains typically cross the border multiple times per day with the same crew aboard. Due to this unique circumstance, CBP subject matter experts believe that international cargo rail carriers would see some time savings as a result of this rule. Currently, the crew of cargo trains must bring a cargo train to a complete stop in order for CBP to inspect the crew on either arrival or departure. The crew then must restart the train's motion, which is a time-consuming process. Under this rule, because CBP would have crew information prior to arrival at the border and likely would have already processed the crew previously ( printed page 64862) that day, CBP could perform the crew inspection while the train is moving slowly, as opposed to at a complete stop. To the extent that this saves cargo rail carriers time at the border, the benefits to cargo rail carriers would be higher than reported in this analysis. CBP does not currently have quantitative estimate of this time savings and requests comment on this subject.
四、安全效益
iv. Security Benefits
旅客、旅客铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人以及公众都将从本拟议规则带来的安全性提升中受益。通过使CBP能够在列车抵达边境之前进行初步审查,CBP可以就每位旅客的通关事宜提供指示。[44] 2008年,一名加拿大公民因在旅客乘客信息系统(APIS)舱单上与恐怖分子甄别数据集(此前称为恐怖分子甄别数据库)完全匹配,而被CBP拦截并拒绝入境。[45] 作为自愿报告计划的一部分所提供的旅客舱单数据,使CBP官员有时间对该人员进行深入调查,并核查舱单中是否存在该人员的任何潜在同行人员。CBP还在列车抵达之前查核了服役记录和公开信息来源,以尽可能多地获取有关该人员的信息。本分析中安全效益仍未量化。由于本规则,CBP将能够对所有通过铁路抵达或离开美国边境的旅客实施与目前对航空和海运旅客相同水平的审查。
Passengers, passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, and the general public would all benefit from the increased security that would result from this proposed rule. By allowing CBP the time to perform an initial vetting before the train arrives at the border, CBP can provide instruction regarding the clearance of each passenger.[44] In 2008, a Canadian citizen was apprehended by CBP and refused entry after an exact Terrorist Screening Dataset (previously known as the Terrorist Screening Database) match on an APIS manifest.[45] The traveler manifest data, provided as part of the voluntary reporting program, allowed CBP officers the time to research the individual extensively and check the manifest for any potential co-travelers of the individual. CBP also checked service records and open sources to find as much information on the individual before the arrival of the train. Security benefits remain unquantified in this analysis. As a result of this rule, CBP will be able to perform the same level of vetting for all travelers arriving or departing the U.S. border by train, as CBP currently does for air and sea.
五、CBP
v. CBP
CBP OFO(美国海关及边境保护局办公室)报告称,目前如果事先未收到旅客或机组人员舱单且无法进行初步审查,六名CBP官员处理一列平均客运列车大约需要一小时(合六人工时),处理一列平均货运列车在边境大约需要五分钟。虽然预计本规则对货运列车的处理时间只会产生微不足道的减少,但可以通过减少列车减速至完全停止再重新加速所花费的时间而节省时间。自愿报告计划的数据显示,本规则可将处理一列客运列车的时间负担减少至半小时,且仅需四名CBP官员(合二人工时)。将每列列车节省的时间(四人工时)乘以表9中每年处理的已提交舱单列车数量,即可得出CBP每年节省的总时间,如表22所示。与旅客节省部分类似,虽然CBP的时间节省适用于所有抵达列车的检查,但只有部分离境列车能为CBP带来节省。对于离境列车,CBP OFO估计有百分之一的离境客运列车被扣留在边境接受CBP检查。为将此项节省货币化,节省的时间乘以CBP官员的全额负担工资率(99.33美元),结果如表23所示。
CBP OFO reports that it currently takes approximately one hour for six CBP officers to process an average passenger train (six person-hours) and five minutes for an average cargo train at the border if no passenger or crew manifest was received beforehand and no initial vetting was possible. While cargo trains are expected to see only a negligible decrease in processing time with this rule, time may be saved from reducing the time spent decelerating the train to a full stop and then bringing the train back up to speed. Data from the voluntary reporting program shows that this rule can reduce the time burden to process a passenger train to half an hour for four CBP officers (two person-hours). Multiplying the time saved per train (four person-hours) by the number of manifested trains processed per year from Table 9 yields the total time saved by CBP annually and is shown in Table 22. Similar to the passenger savings section, while the CBP time savings applies to inspections of all arriving trains, only a portion of departing trains results in savings for CBP. For departing trains, CBP OFO estimates that one percent of departing passenger trains are held at the border for inspection by CBP. To monetize this savings, the time saved is multiplied by the fully loaded wage rate for a CBP officer ($99.33) and the results are shown in Table 23.
表22, , CBP处理客运列车每年节省的时间

Calendar year | Arriving with manifest | Departing with manifest | Total

六、收益汇总
vi. Summary of Benefits
在自愿报告计划期间,客运铁路承运人经历了平均未折现年收益158,884美元,预计由于本规则的实施,相较于无规则或无自愿报告计划的基线,它们将共同获得未折现年收益405,341美元。由于货运铁路承运人未参与自愿报告期,它们在该期间未获得任何收益。由于货运列车上机组人员数量较少,预计货运铁路承运人因本规则带来的旅客处理速度加快而获得的收益微不足道,CBP估计为0美元。在自愿报告计划期间,国际铁路旅客获得了未折现年收益4,240,290美元,预计由于本规则的实施,相较于无规则或无自愿报告计划的基线,他们将获得未折现年收益8,170,861美元。在自愿报告计划期间,由于初步审查,CBP获得了平均未折现年收益459,823美元,预计由于本规则的实施,相较于无规则或无自愿报告计划的基线,CBP将获得未折现年收益701,638美元。
Passenger rail carriers experienced an average undiscounted annual benefit of $158,884 during the voluntary reporting program and are expected to collectively experience an undiscounted annual benefit of $405,341 as a result of this rule compared to a baseline of no rule or voluntary reporting program. Cargo rail carriers experienced no benefits as a result of the voluntary reporting period because they did not participate. Cargo rail carriers are expected to collectively experience only negligible benefits from faster traveler processing as a result of this rule due to the low number of crew aboard cargo trains, and CBP estimates them to be $0. International rail passengers experienced an undiscounted annual benefit of $4,240,290 during the voluntary reporting program and are expected to experience an undiscounted annual benefit of $8,170,861 as a result of this rule compared to a baseline of no rule or voluntary reporting program. CBP experienced an average undiscounted annual benefit of $459,823 as a result of the initial vetting during the voluntary reporting program and is expected to experience an undiscounted annual benefit of $701,638 as a result of this rule compared to a baseline of no rule or voluntary reporting program.
下表第24表显示了本拟议规则对客运铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人、铁路旅客和美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)的折现效益。预计客运铁路承运人在2026年至2030年期间将分别按3%和7%的折现率获得1,802,276美元和1,553,251美元的总效益。客运铁路承运人的年化效益将为405,341美元。预计货运铁路承运人从本规则中获得的效益可忽略不计,因此CBP估计其为0美元。预计铁路旅客在2026年至2030年期间将分别按3%和7%的折现率获得36,330,246美元和31,310,416美元的效益。铁路旅客的年化效益将为8,170,861美元。预计CBP将分别按3%和7%的折现率获得3,119,704美元和2,688,648美元的效益。CBP的年化效益将为701,638美元。
In Table 24 below, the discounted benefits of this proposed rule are shown for passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, rail passengers, and CBP. Passenger rail carriers are expected to experience a total benefit from 2026 to 2030 of $1,802,276 and $1,553,251 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively. Annualized benefits for passenger rail carriers would be $405,341. Cargo rail carriers are expected to experience only negligible benefits from this rule, and therefore CBP estimates them to be $0. From 2026 to 2030, rail passengers are expected to experience a benefit of $36,330,246 and $31,310,416 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively. Annualized benefits for rail passengers would be $8,170,861. CBP is expected to experience a benefit of $3,119,704 and $2,688,648 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively. Annualized benefits for CBP would be $701,638.

下表第25表显示了与2012年至2025年自愿报告期相关的客运铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人、铁路旅客和CBP的折现效益。客运铁路承运人在2012年至2025年期间分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了2,667,560美元和3,440,358美元的总效益。客运铁路承运人分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了160,806美元和163,242美元的年化效益。2012年至2025年期间,货运铁路承运人未获得任何效益, (印刷页 64864) 因为他们未参与自愿报告计划。铁路旅客在自愿报告期内分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了72,566,876美元和96,025,942美元的总效益。铁路旅客分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了4,374,486美元和4,556,343美元的年化效益。CBP分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了7,797,150美元和10,198,237美元的总效益。CBP分别按3%和7%的折现率获得了470,029美元和483,897美元的年化效益。
In Table 25 below, the discounted benefits are shown for passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, rail passengers, and CBP relating to the voluntary reporting period from 2012 through 2025. Passenger rail carriers experienced a total benefit from 2012 to 2025 of $2,667,560 and $3,440,358 using a discount rate of 3-percent and 7-percent respectively. Passenger rail carriers experienced annualized benefits of $160,806 and $163,242 using a discount rate of 3-percent and 7-percent respectively. From 2012 to 2025, cargo rail carriers experienced no benefits as ( printed page 64864) they did not participate in the voluntary reporting program. Rail passengers experienced a total benefit of $72,566,876 and $96,025,942 during the voluntary reporting period using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively. Annualized benefits for rail passengers were $4,374,486 and $4,556,343, using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate, respectively. CBP experienced total benefits of $7,797,150 and $10,198,237 when using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate. Annualized benefits for CBP were $470,029 and $483,897, using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate, respectively.

c. 本规则的净影响
c. Net Impact of Rule
CBP通过从预期收益中减去预期成本来估算本规则的净影响。自愿报告期（2012年至2025年）和监管期（2026年至2030年）的未贴现净收益如下表26所示。客运铁路承运人在参与自愿报告计划期间平均每年获得132,938美元的未贴现净收益，预计因本规则将获得346,911美元的未贴现年净货币化收益。自愿报告计划下乘客的未贴现年净收益为4,240,290美元，预计将增加至8,170,861美元的节省时间收益。货运铁路承运人在自愿报告计划下未获得净收益，预计因本规则将产生未贴现年净收益为负380,495美元（即净成本）。[46] CBP在自愿报告计划下的未贴现年净收益平均为101,317美元。CBP预计因本规则将获得62,567美元的未贴现年收益。
CBP estimated the net impact of the rule by subtracting the expected costs from the expected benefits. Undiscounted net benefits for the voluntary reporting period (2012-2025) and the regulatory period (2026-2030) are shown below in Table 26. Passenger rail carriers experienced an average undiscounted annual net benefit of $132,938 while participating in the voluntary reporting program and are expected to experience undiscounted annual net monetized benefits of $346,911 as a result of this rule. The undiscounted annual net benefits for passengers under the voluntary reporting program were $4,240,290 and are expected to increase to $8,170,861 in time saved. Cargo rail carriers experienced no net benefits under the voluntary reporting program and are expected to experience undiscounted annual net benefits of −$380,495 (a net cost) as a result of this rule.[46] CBP's undiscounted annual net benefits averaged $101,317 under the voluntary reporting program. CBP expects to see undiscounted annual benefits of $62,567 as a result of this rule.

所有铁路承运人、乘客和公众都将因本拟议规则带来的国家安全提升而受益，尽管这仍是一项定性收益。CBP认为，该收益足以证明货运铁路承运人预期承担的成本是合理的。
All rail carriers, passengers, and the general public would benefit from increased national security as a result of this proposed rule though this remains a qualitative benefit. CBP believes that this benefit justifies the expected costs for cargo rail carriers.
表27和表28提供了本规则在2026年至2030年期间的贴现净收益估计值。使用3%和7%的贴现率,客运铁路承运人预计将分别获得1,542,478美元和1,329,350美元的总净收益,年化收益为346,911美元。使用3%和7%的贴现率,货运铁路承运人预计将分别获得负1,691,802美元和负1,458,042美元的净收益,年化净收益为负380,495美元。[47] 使用3%和7%的贴现率,铁路乘客预计将分别获得36,330,246美元和31,310,416美元的净收益,年化净收益为8,170,861美元。美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)预计使用3%和7%的贴现率将分别获得278,193美元和239,754美元的净收益,年化净收益为62,567美元。表29提供了本拟议规则的未贴现净收益、净收益现值以及年化净收益。乘客、客运铁路承运人、货运铁路承运人以及社会公众均将从本拟议规则所带来的安全性提升中受益,尽管这些收益尚未量化。
Table 27 and Table 28 provide estimates of the discounted net benefits of this rule for the time period from 2026 to 2030. Passenger rail carriers are expected to experience a total net benefit of $1,542,478 and $1,329,350 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized benefits of $346,911. Cargo rail carriers are expected to experience net benefits of −$1,691,802 and −$1,458,042 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized net benefits of −$380,495.[47] Rail passengers are expected to experience net benefits of $36,330,246 and $31,310,416 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized net benefits of $8,170,861. CBP expects to experience net benefits of $278,193 and $239,754 using a 3-percent and 7-percent discount rate respectively and annualized net benefits of $62,567. Table 29 provides the undiscounted net benefits, present value of net benefits, and the annualized net benefits of the proposed rule. Passengers, passenger rail carriers, cargo rail carriers, and the general public would all benefit from the increased security that would result from this proposed rule though the benefits remain unquantified.

3. 监管替代方案
3. Regulatory Alternatives
CBP考虑了本规则的两种替代方案,但认定本规则是实现上述安全收益的最佳途径。
CBP considered two alternatives to this rule but determined the rule was the optimal path forward to achieve the security benefits discussed above.
本规则的第一种替代方案是无限期地保留自愿报告计划。该替代方案实际上会将自愿报告计划的成本和收益延续至未来,而不产生任何新的成本或收益。该替代方案被否决,原因是并非所有跨境铁路承运人都参与了该计划,而要实现前述安全收益,CBP需要所有承运人都参与其中。由于承运人可能一直在等待该计划成为强制性要求后才参与,本拟议规则将成为促使承运人开始向CBP提供必要信息以实现本规则安全收益的催化剂。
The first alternative to this rule was to keep the voluntary reporting program in place indefinitely. This alternative would effectively continue the costs and benefits of the voluntary reporting program into the future with no new costs or benefits. This alternative was rejected because not all rail carriers that cross the border are participating in the program and to achieve the security benefits stated earlier, CBP needs all carriers to participate. Since carriers may be withholding their participation until the program becomes mandatory, this proposed rule would be the catalyst carriers need to begin providing the necessary information to CBP to achieve the security benefits of this rule.
该规则的第二种备选方案是仅使对旅客铁路承运人适用本拟议规则中的要求。该备选方案不会给货运铁路承运人带来任何新的成本或收益,但不允许美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)在货运列车抵达之前有时间有效审查旅客信息。该备选方案因不足而被否决,因为需要所有铁路承运人共同参与,才能实现本规则所述的安全效益。
The second alternative to this rule was to subject only passenger rail carriers to the requirements in this proposed rule. This alternative would result in no new costs or benefits to cargo rail carriers but does not allow CBP time to effectively review traveler information on cargo trains before their arrival. This alternative was rejected as insufficient since all rail carriers would need to participate for the stated security benefits of this rule to be achieved.
B. 《行政程序灵活法》
B. Regulatory Flexibility Act
《行政程序灵活法》(5 U.S.C. 601 及其后续条款)(以下简称RFA),经《1996年小企业监管公平法》(SBREFA)修订,要求各机构评估法规对小实体的影响。小实体可以是小企业(定义为根据《小企业法》符合小企业资格、独立拥有和经营且在其领域不占主导地位的任何企业);小型非营利组织;或小型政府管辖区(人口少于50,000人的地方)。
The Regulatory Flexibility Act (5 U.S.C. 601 et seq.) (RFA), as amended by the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (SBREFA), requires agencies to assess the impact of regulations on small entities. A small entity may be a small business (defined as any independently owned and operated business not dominant in its field that qualifies as a small business per the Small Business Act); a small not-for-profit organization; or a small governmental jurisdiction (locality with fewer than 50,000 people).
CBP确定有七家受本拟议规则影响的小型美国货运铁路承运人。其中规模最小的公司年收入为155,000美元,七家公司的年收入中位数为2,576,000美元。CBP认为,实施本规则不会给小型货运承运人带来重大成本。将每列货运列车所需文件的时间负担总和(共计0.1小时,即六分钟)乘以机车工程师的工资率(每小时54.19美元),可得出每列货运列车跨越美国边境的成本,即5.42美元。按此计算,规模最小的企业需要286次过境才能使成本达到其收入的约百分之一,而中位数规模的小企业则需要5,122次过境。CBP已确定这不会对小型货运铁路承运人构成重大成本,但就此征求意见。
CBP identified seven small U.S.-based cargo rail carriers that would be affected by this proposed rule. The smallest of these companies has an annual revenue of $155,000 and the median annual revenue of the seven companies is $2,576,000. CBP believes that implementation of this rule would not result in a significant cost to small cargo carriers. Using the sum of the time burdens for documents that would be required for each cargo train (a total of 0.1 hours or six minutes) and multiplying by the wage rate for a locomotive engineer ($54.19) provides the cost per cargo train crossing the U.S. border which is $5.42. At this rate, it would take 286 crossings for the smallest business to experience costs of around one percent of their revenue and 5,122 crossings for the median small business. CBP has determined this would not constitute a significant cost to small cargo rail carriers but requests comment on this.
目前没有会受本拟议规则影响的小型美国旅客铁路承运人。
There are currently no small U.S.-based passenger rail carriers that would be affected by this proposed rule.
CBP证明,拟议规则如获颁布,不会对任何美国小型企业产生重大经济影响。
CBP certifies that the proposed rule, if promulgated, would not have a significant economic impact to any U.S.-based small businesses.
C. 《文书工作削减法》
C. Paperwork Reduction Act
本文件中的信息收集事项将根据《文书工作削减法》(44 U.S.C. 3507)的要求,在控制编号1651-0088下提交管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查。除非信息收集显示OMB分配的有效控制编号,否则机构不得进行信息收集,个人也无需对信息收集作出回应。本次规则制定的信息收集事项已纳入自动化信息收集系统(APIS)的现有收集项目中,OMB控制编号为1651-0088。
The collections of information in this document will be submitted for OMB review in accordance with the requirements of the Paperwork Reduction Act (44 U.S.C. 3507) under control number 1651-0088. An agency may not conduct, and a person is not required to respond to, a collection of information unless the collection of information displays a valid control number assigned by OMB. The collections of information for this rulemaking are included in an existing collection for the automated information collection system, APIS, under the OMB control number 1651-0088.
经OMB批准的收集项目1651-0088将被修订,以反映新增的旅客铁路承运人和货运铁路承运人受访者。我们估计,本规则将每年新增141,381份回复和6,512个额外负担小时。信息收集的修订将包括10,491份商业旅客铁路提交文件,这将是此前批准的每年9,540份提交文件总数的增加。该修订还将为货运铁路承运人设立新的提交要求。本次信息收集修订将包括每年140,430份新的货运铁路提交文件。
OMB-approved collection 1651-0088 would be amended to reflect the additional passenger rail carrier and cargo rail carrier respondents. We estimate that this rule would result in an additional 141,381 responses annually and 6,512 additional burden hours. The revision to the information collection would include 10,491 commercial passenger rail submissions, which would be an increase of the previously approved 9,540 total submissions annually. It would also establish new submission requirements for cargo rail carriers. This revision to this information collection would include 140,430 new cargo rail submissions annually.
意见应按照本拟议规则制定说明性文本中列出的指示提交给CBP,并注明OMB控制编号1651-0088。意见应针对以下方面:(a)信息收集对于该机构正确履行职能是否必要,包括该信息是否具有实际效用;(b)该机构对信息收集负担的估计的准确性,包括所使用方法和假设的有效性;(c)提高拟收集信息的质量、效用和清晰度的方式;(d)尽量减少对需作出回应者负担的方式,包括使用自动化收集技术或其他形式的信息技术;以及(e)受访者或记录保存者因信息收集而产生的年度成本负担(总资本/启动成本以及运营和维护成本)。
Comments should be submitted to CBP and include the OMB Control Number 1651-0088 per the instructions outlined in the introductory text of this proposed rulemaking. The comments should address: (a) whether the collection of information is necessary for the proper performance of the functions of the agency, including whether the information shall have practical utility; (b) the accuracy of the agency's estimates of the burden of the collection of information, including the validity of the methodology and assumptions used; (c) ways to enhance the quality, utility, and clarity of the information to be collected; (d) ways to minimize the burden on those who are to respond, including the use of automated collection techniques or the use of other forms of information technology; and (e) the annual cost burden to respondents or record keepers form the collection of information (total capital/startup costs and operations and maintenance costs).
OMB征集编号1651-0088,《载客航班旅客及机组人员舱单》,将予以修订,以反映新增旅客铁路承运人申报和货运铁路申报所增加的负担时数,总计预计年度答复总数为198,821,756份,年度负担时数为1,004,608小时,具体如下:[48]

D. 《无资金拨款授权改革法》
D. Unfunded Mandates Reform Act
1995年3月22日颁布为第104-4号公共法律的《1995年无资金拨款授权改革法》第二章规定,各联邦机构应在法律允许的范围内,就拟议或最终机构规则中可能导致州、地方及部落政府合计或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上（每年根据通货膨胀进行调整）的任何联邦强制性规定,编制书面影响评估报告。 参见《美国法典》第2编第1532(a)条。本规则不会导致州、地方及部落政府合计或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上。因此,根据《1995年无资金拨款授权改革法》的规定,无需采取任何行动。
Title II of the Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995, enacted as Public Law 104-4 on March 22, 1995, requires each Federal agency, to the extent permitted by law, to prepare a written assessment of the effects of any Federal mandate in a proposed or final agency rule that may result in the expenditure by state, local, and tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector, of $100 million or more (adjusted annually for inflation) in any one year. See2 U.S.C. 1532(a). This rule will not result in the expenditure by state, local, and tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector, of $100 million or more in any one year. Therefore, no actions are necessary under the provisions of the Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995.
E. 《隐私法》
E. Privacy Act
根据本规则要求传输的信息,将按照1974年《隐私法》（《美国法典》第5编第552a条,以下简称《隐私法》）的所有要求,保存在美国海关及边境保护局（CBP）系统中。若本规则定稿,美国海关及边境保护局（CBP）将发布或更新任何必要的隐私影响评估和/或隐私法记录系统通知,以全面说明确保符合《隐私法》保护要求的流程。与APIS系统以及铁路承运人自愿传输APIS数据相关的现行《隐私法》合规文件,可在以下网址查阅: https://www.dhs.gov/​publication/​advanced-passenger-information-system-apis-update-national-counterterrorism-center-nctc。另请参见上文脚注1。
The information required to be transmitted under this rule would be retained in CBP systems in a manner consistent with all requirements of the Privacy Act of 1974, 5 U.S.C. 552a (Privacy Act). If this rule is finalized, CBP will issue or update any necessary Privacy Impact Assessment and/or Privacy Act System of Records Notice(s) to fully outline processes that will ensure compliance with Privacy Act protections. Current Privacy Act compliance documents related to the APIS system and the voluntary transmission of APIS data by rail carriers are available at https://www.dhs.gov/​publication/​advanced-passenger-information-system-apis-update-national-counterterrorism-center-nctc. See also footnote 1, above.
F. 第14294号行政命令
F. Executive Order 14294
第14294号行政命令（《打击联邦法规过度刑事化》）旨在促进拟议或最终规则的监管透明度,凡违反该等规则可能构成刑事监管罪行者均适用之。根据第14294号行政命令第8节,该命令不适用于移民法律或法规的执行,也不适用于与国家安全或国防相关的法律或法规的执行。本拟议规则涉及移民法律的执行,并与国家安全相关。因此,第14294号行政命令不适用。
Executive Order 14294 (Fighting Overcriminalization in Federal Regulations) aims to promote regulatory transparency for proposed or final rules, the violation of which may constitute a criminal regulatory offense. Pursuant to section 8 of Executive Order 14294, the order does not apply to the enforcement of the immigration laws or regulations or to the enforcement of laws or regulations related to national security or defense. This proposed rule involves enforcement of the immigration laws and is related to national security. Accordingly, Executive Order 14294 does not apply.

The signing authority for these amendments falls under 19 CFR 0.2(a). Accordingly, this document is signed by the Secretary of Homeland Security (or the Secretary's delegate).

3. 修订并重新公布§ 123.6,内容如下:
3. Revise and republish § 123.6 to read as follows:
§ 123.6
§ 123.6
到达火车的列车清单。
Train Sheet for arriving railroad trains.
从加拿大或墨西哥到达的火车列车长应向到达口岸的CBP官员出示各车厢清单和列车清单,该清单有时也称为编组单、桥梁单或行程单,列明每节车厢并显示车厢号码和首字母缩写。但是,如承运人已按照本部分子部分K所要求的规定传输信息,则从加拿大或墨西哥到达且未载有商业货物的火车列车长无需出示各车厢清单或列车清单。
The conductor of a railroad train arriving from Canada or Mexico shall present to the CBP officer at the port of arrival individual car manifests and a train sheet, sometimes called a consist, bridge sheet, or trip sheet, listing each car and showing the car numbers and initials. However, a conductor of a railroad train arriving from Canada or Mexico without commercial cargo on board is not required to present individual car manifests or a train sheet if the carrier has transmitted the information in accordance with the provisions required by subpart K of this part.

§ 123.100
§ 123.100
定义。
Definitions.
就本子部分而言,适用以下定义:
For purposes of this subpart, the following definitions apply:
列车长 指负责管理列车的铁路员工,同时也是列车乘务组的直接主管。
Conductor means the railway employee charged with the management of a train and is also the direct supervisor of the train's crew.
乘务组 指善意地以列车正常运行和行程服务所需的任何身份在列车上服勤的任何人员,包括列车长和司机。
Crew means any person serving on board a train in good faith in any capacity required for the normal operation and service of the trip including the conductor and engineer.
离开 和 离境 指列车车门关闭,列车在跨越美国边境的进境或出境行程中离开最后一个车站或停靠点的时间点。
Depart and Departure mean the point at which the train doors are closed, and the train leaves the last station or stop prior to crossing the U.S. border on either an inbound or outbound trip.
DHS批准的旅行证件 指经美国国土安全部批准用于出入美国旅行的旅行证件,例如护照或其他经西半球旅行倡议(WHTI)批准的证件。
DHS-approved travel document means a travel document approved by the U.S. Department of Homeland Security for travel in or out of the United States, such as a passport or other Western Hemisphere Travel Initiative (WHTI) approved document.
紧急情况 指因机械、医疗或安全问题、自然或人为灾害、因化学、生物或放射性事件而进行的疏散,或运送应急人员前往此类事件现场而导致的紧急状况。
Emergency means an urgent situation due to a mechanical, medical, or security problem, natural or manmade disasters, evacuations due to chemical, biological, or radiological events, or the transport of emergency responders to such incidents.
司机 指负责并对列车机械操作承担责任的一人或多人。
Engineer means the individual(s) in charge of and responsible for the mechanical operation of a train.
乘客 指在列车上运送的任何人员,包括偷乘者,但不符合乘务组定义的人员。
Passenger means any person being transported on a train, including stowaways, who does not meet the definition of crew.
车站 指火车定期停靠以装卸旅客或货物的铁路设施。
Station means a railroad facility where trains regularly stop to load or unload passengers or goods.
停靠点 指火车在车站之间可装卸旅客或货物的铁路设施,或火车仅根据信号停车的铁路设施,即所谓的招手停车点。
Stop means a railroad facility where trains can load or unload passengers or goods between stations or a railroad facility where the train stops only on a signal, known as a flag stop.
列车 指用于商业或私人目的提供铁路运输服务的任何运输工具,包括货运工具和客运工具。
Train means any conveyance being used to provide railroad transportation for commercial or private purposes, including freight conveyances and passenger conveyances.
旅客 指将在列车上跨越美国边境的任何个人,包括乘客和机组人员,该列车将从外国地点抵达美国,或从美国出发前往外国地点,包括在过境途中将跨越美国边境的列车。
Traveler means any individual, including passengers and crew, who will cross the U.S. border while on board a train that will arrive in the United States from a foreign location or depart the United States for a foreign location, including a train that will cross the U.S. border while in transit.
美国 指美国本土,并包括阿拉斯加州。
United States means the continental United States and includes Alaska.
第123.101条
§ 123.101
所有载有旅客的列车抵达或离境的提前通知。
Advance notice of arrival or departure for all trains with a traveler on board.
(a) 一般要求。 除适用第123.105条规定的美国军方豁免情形外,铁路承运人必须以电子方式向美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)传输抵达或离境提前通知,提供本条第(c)款所列的舱单信息,适用于任何载有旅客的列车,该列车从外国地点抵达美国或从美国出发前往外国地点,包括在过境途中跨越美国边境的任何列车。本条规定的电子传输必须依照第123.104条进行。铁路承运人可以授权另一方代表其提交抵达或离境通知。但是,铁路承运人在任何时候均须对所传输信息的传输、准确性、及时性和完整性负责。
(a) General requirements. A rail carrier must electronically transmit to CBP an advance notice of arrival or departure providing the manifest information listed in paragraph (c) of this section for any train with a traveler on board arriving in the United States from a foreign location or departing the United States for a foreign location, including any train that crosses the U.S. border while in transit, unless the U.S. military exemption under § 123.105 applies. The electronic transmissions under this section must be made in accordance with § 123.104. The rail carrier may authorize another party to submit the notice of arrival or departure on the rail carrier's behalf. However, the rail carrier is responsible at all times for the transmission, accuracy, timeliness, and completeness of the transmitted information.
(b) 传输截止时间。 铁路承运人必须在列车抵达美国边境口岸之前不少于24小时,以电子方式传输入境列车的抵达通知或出境列车的离境通知,提供本条第(c)款所列的信息。
(b) Deadline for transmission. A rail carrier must electronically transmit the notice of arrival for an inbound train or the notice of departure for an outbound train providing the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section no later than 24 hours prior to the train's arrival at a U.S. border crossing location.
(c) 所需的列车及行程数据。 铁路承运人必须传输构成抵达或离境通知的以下信息:
(c) Train and trip data required. The rail carrier must transmit the following information constituting the notice of arrival or departure:
(1) 铁路承运人代码(由CBP提供);
(1) Rail carrier's code (as provided by CBP);
(2) 行程编号;
(2) Trip number;
(3) 列车编号;
(3) Train number;
(4) 车厢编号,按顺序排列(如有多节车厢);
(4) Car numbers in order, if more than one car;
(5) 始发地(或由CBP提供的代码);
(5) Place of origination (or code as provided by CBP);
(6) 列车预计从始发站或停靠站出发的当地时间和日期;
(6) Estimated local time and date that the train will leave from the origination station or stop;
(7) 美国边境口岸(或由CBP提供的代码);
(7) Place of U.S. border crossing (or code as provided by CBP);
(8) 预计抵达美国边境口岸的当地时间和日期;
(8) Estimated local time and date of arrival at the U.S. border crossing location;
(9) 最终目的地(或由CBP提供的代码);
(9) Place of final destination (or code as provided by CBP);
(10) 预计抵达最终目的地的当地时间和日期;
(10) Estimated local time and date of arrival at the final destination;
(11) 完整行程安排,包括所有预定停靠站;
(11) Complete itinerary, including all scheduled stops;
(12) 24小时紧急联系人信息,包括铁路承运人方面的全名和电话号码(必须是熟悉该特定行程的个人);以及
(12) Information for a 24-hour emergency point of contact consisting of full name and phone number for the rail carrier (must be an individual who is knowledgeable about the particular trip); and
(13) 铁路承运人的名称和地址。
(13) Rail carrier's name and address.
(d) 抵达或离境提前通知的变更 , ,
(d) Changes to the advance notice of arrival or departure —
(1) 传输变更的要求。 如果本条第(c)款所列信息中的任何一项在传输至CBP之后、列车跨越美国边境之前发生变更,铁路承运人必须将新的或修订后的信息传输给CBP。新的或修订后的信息必须在列车离开铁路承运人收到该新信息或修订信息所在的车站或停靠站之前传输;如果信息是在两站之间收到的,则必须不迟于列车离开收到该信息后的下一个车站或停靠站时传输。如果铁路承运人在列车出发之后、但在跨越美国边境之前收到新的或修订后的信息,则铁路承运人必须在跨越美国边境之前将该更新信息传输给CBP。
(1) Requirement to transmit changes. If any of the information enumerated in paragraph (c) of this section changes after transmission to CBP and prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, the rail carrier must transmit the new or revised information to CBP. The new or revised information must be transmitted prior to the train leaving the station or stop at which the rail carrier received the new or revised information or, if the information was received between stations or stops, no later than when the train leaves the next station or stop after receiving the information. If the rail carrier receives new or revised information after departure but prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, then the rail carrier must transmit such updated information to CBP prior to crossing the U.S. border.
(2) 边境过境时间的变更。 如果预计抵达美国边境口岸的时间发生30分钟或以上的变更,铁路承运人除须依照本条第(d)(1)款的规定传输更新后的预计抵达时间外,还必须联系该边境口岸的CBP,以确认CBP将有可用资源对列车进行检查。承运人必须在出发前联系CBP,如果变更是在出发后发生的,则必须在抵达美国边境口岸之前联系CBP。
(2) Changes to border crossing time. If there is a change of 30 minutes or more to the estimated time of arrival at the U.S. border crossing location, the rail carrier must, in addition to transmitting the updated estimated time of arrival as required by paragraph (d)(1) of this section, contact CBP at the border crossing location to confirm that CBP resources will be available to inspect the train. The carrier must contact CBP prior to departure or, if the change arises after departure, prior to arrival at the U.S. border crossing location.
(印刷页第64869页)
( printed page 64869)
第123.102条
§ 123.102
所有搭乘列车抵达美国的旅客所需的提前电子信息。
Advance electronic information required for all travelers arriving in the United States on board a train.
(a) 一般要求。 铁路承运人必须以电子方式向美国海关及边境保护局(CBP)传输本条第(c)款所列的预先信息,涉及所有乘坐自境外地点抵达美国的列车的旅客,包括任何过境期间穿越美国边境的列车,除非第123.105条规定的美国军事豁免适用。本条规定的电子传输必须依照第123.104条进行。铁路承运人可以授权另一方代表铁路承运人传输所需信息。但是,铁路承运人在任何时候均对所传输信息的传输、准确性、及时性和完整性负责。
(a) General requirements. A rail carrier must electronically transmit to CBP the advance information listed in paragraph (c) of this section for all travelers on board a train arriving in the United States from a foreign location, including any train that crosses the U.S. border while in transit, unless the U.S. military exemption under § 123.105 applies. The electronic transmissions under this section must be made in accordance with § 123.104. The rail carrier may authorize another party to transmit the required information on the rail carrier's behalf. However, the rail carrier is responsible at all times for the transmission, accuracy, timeliness, and completeness of the transmitted information.
(b) 传输截止期限。 铁路承运人必须不迟于列车发车前60分钟传输本条第(c)款所列信息。
(b) Deadline for transmission. A rail carrier must transmit the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section no later than 60 minutes prior to departure.
(c) 所需旅客数据。 铁路承运人必须传输列车上每位旅客的以下信息:
(c) Traveler data required. The rail carrier must transmit the following information for each traveler on board the train:
(1) 全名(姓、名,如有可用则包括中间名);
(1) Full name (last, first, and, if available, middle);
(2) 出生日期;
(2) Date of birth;
(3) 性别;
(3) Sex;
(4) 国籍国;
(4) Country of citizenship;
(5) 居住国;
(5) Country of residence;
(6) 美国国土安全部(DHS)批准的旅行证件类型(如需要美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件);
(6) DHS-approved travel document type, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(7) 美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件号码(如需要美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件);
(7) DHS-approved travel document number, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(8) 美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件签发国(如需要美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件);
(8) DHS-approved travel document country of issuance, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(9) 美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件有效期(如适用,且如需要美国国土安全部批准的旅行证件);
(9) DHS-approved travel document expiration date, if applicable, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(10) 旅客在列车上的身份(即,列车长、司机、乘客、乘务人员);
(10) Traveler's status on board the train ( i.e., conductor, engineer, passenger, crew);
(11) 旅客的完整行程,包括起始站和目的站或停靠站;
(11) Complete itinerary of traveler, including origination and designation station or stop;
(12) 在美国期间的地址(门牌号和街道、城市、州和邮政编码),但对于过境前往美国以外地点的旅客,不要求提供此信息;
(12) Address while in the United States (number and street, city, state, and zip code), except this information is not required for travelers who are in transit to a location outside the United States;
(13) 外国人登记号码(如适用);
(13) Alien registration number, if applicable;
(14) 旅客的旅客姓名记录定位码(如有);
(14) Traveler's passenger name record locator, if available;
(15) 已知旅客号码(如适用);
(15) Known traveler number, if applicable;
(16) 救济号码(如适用);
(16) Redress number, if applicable;
(17) 行李标签信息(如适用);
(17) Baggage tag information, if applicable;
(18) 座位位置,包括车厢和座位号(如有);
(18) Seat location, including car and seat number, if available;
(19) 联系电话号码,包括国家代码。承运人须向旅客索取附有国家代码的电话号码,如旅客提供,承运人必须将其传输给美国海关及边境保护局;以及
(19) Contact phone number, including country code. The carrier is required to request a phone number with country code from the traveler, and if the traveler provides one, the carrier must transmit it to CBP.; and
(20) 电子邮件地址。承运人须向旅客索取电子邮件地址,如旅客提供,承运人必须将其传输给美国海关及边境保护局。
(20) Email address. The carrier is required to request an email address from the traveler, and if the traveler provides one, the carrier must transmit it to CBP.
(d) 美国海关及边境保护局的回复信息及要求。 美国海关及边境保护局将对所传输的旅客信息进行审查,并向铁路承运人传输一条回复信息。该回复信息将说明美国海关及边境保护局已接受该传输,或说明铁路承运人必须在发车前与美国海关及边境保护局联系。
(d) CBP response message and requirements. CBP will conduct vetting of the transmitted traveler information and will transmit a response message to the rail carrier. The response message will state either that CBP accepted the transmission or that the rail carrier must contact CBP prior to departure.
(e) 旅客数据的变更,
(e) Changes to traveler data—
(1) 传输变更信息的要求。 每当本条第(c)款所列信息在传输给美国海关及边境保护局之后,且在列车穿越美国边境之前发生变更时,包括旅客在列车穿越美国边境之前上车或下车的情形,铁路承运人必须以电子方式传输新信息或修订信息。铁路承运人必须不迟于列车离开收到新信息或修订信息所在车站或停靠站时传输该新信息或修订信息。如果铁路承运人在两站或两个停靠站之间收到新信息或修订信息,铁路承运人必须在离开下一个车站或停靠站之前以电子方式传输该新信息或修订信息。如果铁路承运人在发车后但在列车穿越美国边境之前收到新信息或修订信息,则铁路承运人必须在列车穿越美国边境之前将该新信息或修订信息以电子方式传输给美国海关及边境保护局。
(1) Requirement to transmit changes. Each time any of the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section changes after transmission to CBP and prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, including if travelers get on or off the train prior to crossing the U.S. border, the rail carrier must electronically transmit the new or revised information. The rail carrier must transmit the new or revised information no later than when the train leaves the station or stop during which the rail carrier received the new or revised information. If the rail carrier receives new or revised information between stations or stops, the rail carrier must electronically transmit the new or revised information prior to departing the next station or stop. If the rail carrier receives the new or revised information after departure but prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, then the rail carrier must electronically transmit the new or revised information to CBP prior to crossing the U.S. border.
(2) 须发出新的回复信息。 在收到本条第(e)(1)款要求的新信息或修订信息后,美国海关及边境保护局将对更新后的信息进行审查,并向铁路承运人传输一条回复信息。该回复信息将说明美国海关及边境保护局已接受该传输,或说明铁路承运人必须在发车前与美国海关及边境保护局联系,或者如果铁路承运人是在发车后收到新信息或修订信息,则须在列车穿越美国边境之前与美国海关及边境保护局联系。
(2) New response message required. Upon receipt of the new or revised information required by paragraph (e)(1) of this section, CBP will vet the updated information and will transmit a response message to the rail carrier. The response message will state either that CBP has accepted the transmission or that the rail carrier must contact CBP prior to departure, or prior to the train crossing the U.S. border if the new or revised information was received by the rail carrier after departure.
(f) 铁路承运人负责将所收集的信息与旅行证件进行核对的责任。 收集本节第(c)段所列信息的铁路承运人,负责将旅客出示的旅行证件与其向美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)传输的旅行证件信息进行核对,以确保该信息与旅行证件上所载信息相符,证件在外观上看似可用于旅行目的,且该旅客即为该旅行证件的持有人。
(f) Rail carrier responsibility for comparing information collected with travel documents. The rail carrier collecting the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section is responsible for comparing the travel documents presented by the traveler with the travel document information it is transmitting to CBP to ensure that such information matches the information contained on the travel documents, the documents appear to be valid for travel purposes, and the traveler is the person to whom the travel documents were issued.
第123.103条
§ 123.103
所有乘火车离开美国的旅客均须提供的预先电子信息。
Advance electronic information required for all travelers departing from the United States on board a train.
(a) 一般要求。 铁路承运人必须以电子方式向CBP传输本节第(c)段所列的预先信息,涉及所有乘坐自美国出发前往美国境外任何地点的火车的旅客,包括在过境途中穿越美国边境的任何列车,除非符合第123.105条规定的美国军方豁免情形。本条项下的电子传输必须按照第123.104条的规定进行。铁路承运人可以授权其他方代表其提交所需信息。但是,铁路承运人在任何时候均须对所传输信息的传输、准确性、及时性和完整性负责。
(a) General requirement. A rail carrier must electronically transmit to CBP the advance information listed in paragraph (c) of this section for all travelers on board a train departing from the United States for any location outside the United States, including any train that crosses the U.S. border while in transit, unless the U.S. military exemption under § 123.105 applies. The electronic transmissions under this section must be made in accordance with § 123.104. The rail carrier may authorize another party to submit the required information on the rail carrier's behalf. However, the rail carrier is responsible at all times for the transmission, accuracy, timeliness, and completeness of the transmitted information.
(b) 传输截止时间。 铁路承运人必须最迟在发车前60分钟传输本节第(c)段所列的信息。
(b) Deadline for transmission. A rail carrier must transmit the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section no later than 60 minutes prior to departure.
(c) 所需的旅客数据。 铁路承运人必须传输列车上每位旅客的下列信息：
(c) Traveler data required. The rail carrier must transmit the following information for each traveler on board the train:
(1) 全名（姓氏、名字,如有中间名也须提供）；
(1) Full name (last, first, and, if available, middle);
(2) 出生日期；
(2) Date of birth;
(3) 性别；
(3) Sex;
(4) 国籍；
(4) Country of citizenship;
(5) 居住国；
(5) Country of residence;
(6) 美国国土安全部（DHS）批准的旅行证件类型，如要求提供DHS批准的旅行证件；
(6) DHS-approved travel document type, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(7) DHS批准的旅行证件号码，如要求提供DHS批准的旅行证件；
(7) DHS-approved travel document number, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(8) DHS批准的旅行证件签发国，如要求提供DHS批准的旅行证件；
(8) DHS-approved travel document country of issuance, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(9) DHS批准的旅行证件有效期（如适用），如要求提供DHS批准的旅行证件；
(9) DHS-approved travel document expiration date, if applicable, if a DHS-approved travel document is required;
(10) 旅客在列车上的身份（即列车长、司机、乘务人员、乘客）；
(10) Traveler's status on board the train ( i.e., conductor, engineer, crew, passenger);
(11) 旅客的完整行程，包括始发站和目的站或停靠站；
(11) Complete itinerary of traveler, including origination and destination station or stop;
(12) 在美国期间的地址（门牌号和街道、城市、州和邮政编码），但对于过境前往美国境外地点的旅客，不要求提供此信息；
(12) Address while in the United States (number and street, city, state, and zip code), except this information is not required for travelers who are in transit to a location outside the United States;
(13) 外国人登记号码（如适用）； （印刷页第64870页）
(13) Alien registration number, if applicable; ( printed page 64870)
(14) 旅客的乘客姓名记录定位码（如有）；
(14) Traveler's passenger name record locator, if available;
(15) 已知旅行者号码（如适用）；
(15) Known traveler number, if applicable;
(16) 申诉号码（如适用）；
(16) Redress number, if applicable;
(17) 行李标签信息（如适用）；
(17) Baggage tag information, if applicable;
(18) 座位位置，包括车厢号和座位号（如有）；
(18) Seat location, including car and seat number, if available;
(19) 联系电话号码，包括国家代码。承运人须向旅客索取附有国家代码的电话号码，如旅客提供该号码，承运人必须将其传输给CBP；以及
(19) Contact phone number, including country code. The carrier is required to request a phone number with country code from the traveler, and if the traveler provides one, the carrier must transmit it to CBP; and
(20) 电子邮件地址。承运人须向旅客索取电子邮件地址，如旅客提供该地址，承运人必须将其传输给CBP。
(20) Email address. The carrier is required to request an email address from the traveler, and if the traveler provides one, the carrier must transmit it to CBP.
(d) CBP的回复信息及要求。 CBP将对所传输的旅客信息进行审核，并向铁路承运人发送回复信息。回复信息将说明CBP已接受该次传输，或说明铁路承运人须在发车前与CBP联系。
(d) CBP response message and requirements. CBP will conduct vetting of the transmitted traveler information and will transmit a response message to the rail carrier. The response message will state either that CBP has accepted the transmission or that the rail carrier must contact CBP prior to departure.
(e) 旅客数据的变更, , , ,
(e) Changes to traveler data—
(1) 传输变更的要求。 在向美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)传输本条第(c)款所列信息之后,以及列车越过美国边境之前,如果该等信息中的任何一项发生变化,包括旅客在列车越过美国边境之前上车或下车的情形,铁路承运人必须以电子方式传输新的或修订后的信息。铁路承运人必须在列车离开接收到新的或修订信息所在车站或停靠点之前,传输该新的或修订后的信息,且不得迟于列车离开该车站或停靠点的时间。如果铁路承运人在两站或两个停靠点之间收到新的或修订后的信息,铁路承运人必须在离开下一个车站或停靠点之前以电子方式传输该新的或修订后的信息。如果铁路承运人在列车发车之后,但在列车越过美国边境之前收到新的或修订后的信息,则铁路承运人必须在列车越过美国边境之前向CBP传输更新后的信息。
(1) Requirement to transmit changes. Each time any of the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section changes after transmission to CBP and prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, including if travelers get on or off the train prior to crossing the U.S. border, the rail carrier must electronically transmit the new or revised information. The rail carrier must transmit the new or revised information no later than when the train leaves the station or stop during which the rail carrier received the new or revised information. If the rail carrier receives new or revised information between stations or stops, the rail carrier must electronically transmit the new or revised information prior to departing the next station or stop. If the rail carrier receives new or revised information after departure, but prior to the train crossing the U.S. border, then the rail carrier must transmit the updated information to CBP prior to crossing the U.S. border.
(2) 须发出新的回应消息。 在收到本条第(e)(1)款所要求的新的或修订后的信息后,CBP将对更新后的信息进行核查,并向铁路承运人传输一条回应消息。该回应消息将说明CBP已接受该次传输,或者说明如果铁路承运人是在发车之后收到新的或修订后的信息,则铁路承运人必须在发车之前或列车越过美国边境之前联系CBP。
(2) New response message required. Upon receipt of the new or revised information required by paragraph (e)(1) of this section, CBP will vet the updated information and will transmit a response message to the rail carrier. The response message will state either that CBP has accepted the transmission or that the rail carrier must contact CBP prior to departure or prior to the train crossing the U.S. border if the new or revised information was received by the rail carrier after departure.
(f) 铁路承运人对比对所收集信息与旅行证件的责任。 收集本条第(c)款所列信息的铁路承运人,负责将旅客出示的旅行证件与其向CBP传输的旅行证件信息进行比对,以确保该等信息与旅行证件所载信息相符,所持证件在表面上看来可用于旅行目的,且该旅客即为该旅行证件所签发的对象本人。
(f) Rail carrier responsibility for comparing information collected with travel documents. The rail carrier collecting the information listed in paragraph (c) of this section is responsible for comparing the travel documents presented by the traveler with the travel document information it is transmitting to CBP in order to ensure that such information matches the information contained on the travel documents, the documents appear to be valid for travel purposes, and the traveler is the person to whom the travel documents were issued.
第123.104条
§ 123.104
向CBP的传输。
Transmission to CBP.
铁路承运人必须依照本条第(a)款或第(b)款之规定,传输本子部分所要求的预先电子信息,但在出现不可预见情形并依照第(c)款的规定允许提交的情况下除外,具体如下:
A rail carrier must transmit the required advance electronic information pursuant to this subpart in accordance with either paragraph (a) or (b) of this section, except when an unforeseen circumstance allows for submission under paragraph (c), as follows:
(a) 经认证电子传输系统选项。 铁路承运人在依照本条第(a)(1)款的规定取得CBP对某一电子传输系统的认证后,可通过该经认证的电子传输系统进行传输,该系统须配置为能够向CBP传输数据并能够从CBP接收相应的消息。
(a) Certified electronic transmission system option. A rail carrier, upon obtaining CBP certification for an electronic transmission system in accordance with paragraph (a)(1) of this section, may make transmissions by means of the certified electronic transmission system configured to transmit data to CBP and receive from CBP appropriate messages.
(1) 在依照本条第(a)款进行任何必需的预先电子传输之前,铁路承运人必须将其电子传输系统提交CBP进行测试,且CBP必须认证该铁路承运人的系统具备与CBP系统进行有效数据传输及接收相应消息通信的能力。
(1) Before making any required advance electronic transmissions under paragraph (a) of this section, a rail carrier must subject its electronic transmission system to CBP for testing and CBP must certify that the rail carrier's system is capable of communicating with the CBP system for effective transmissions of data and receipt of appropriate messages.
(2) 该电子传输系统可配置为通过交互式单项提交、交互式批量提交或非交互式批量提交的方式发送和接收数据。如经认证可同时使用两种方式,承运人在传输信息时可同时使用交互式批量传输和交互式单项传输。
(2) Such electronic transmission system may be configured to send and receive data either through interactive individual submissions, interactive batch submissions, or non-interactive batch submissions. If certified to do so, the carrier may use both an interactive batch transmission and interactive individual transmissions when transmitting information.
(3) 如果铁路承运人使用交互式传输方式,铁路承运人必须在发车后不迟于30分钟内向CBP传输一条消息,列明所有已登车的旅客。该消息必须通过铁路承运人指定的唯一识别码或具体的旅客数据( 例如, 全名)来识别旅客。
(3) If a rail carrier uses an interactive transmission method, the rail carrier must transmit to CBP a message no later than 30 minutes after departure listing all travelers who boarded the train. The message must identify the travelers by a unique identifier assigned by the rail carrier or by specific passenger data ( e.g., full name).
(b) 经CBP批准的电子数据交换系统选项。 铁路承运人可通过任何经CBP批准的电子数据交换系统进行传输。
(b) Approved CBP electronic data interchange system option. A rail carrier may make transmissions by any CBP-approved electronic data interchange system.
(c) 不可预见情形下的替代方案。 在有限的个案基础上,经铁路承运人请求,当不可预见的情形导致无法依照本条(a)款或(b)款以电子方式传输所需信息时,海关与边境保护局(CBP)可允许铁路承运人以电话方式或通过其他经批准的替代程序提交所需信息。在此类情形下,CBP将手动录入铁路承运人提供的信息,对信息进行核查,并向承运人发送回复消息。在此类情形下,CBP的回复消息可通过电子方式、电话方式或其他替代程序接收。
(c) Alternatives under unforeseen circumstances. On a limited case-by-case basis and upon request by a rail carrier, CBP may permit a rail carrier to submit the required information telephonically or through another approved alternative procedure when unforeseen circumstances preclude transmission of the information electronically under paragraph (a) or (b) of this section. Under such circumstances, CBP will manually enter the information provided by the rail carrier, vet the information, and send a response message to the carrier. CBP's response message may be received electronically, telephonically, or through another alternative procedure in such cases.
第123.105条
§ 123.105
美国军方豁免。
U.S. military exemption.
对于持命令乘坐美国战争部包租列车的现役美国军事人员,本分部不要求提供任何信息。
No information is required under this subpart for active-duty U.S. military personnel traveling on orders as travelers on board a U.S. Department of War-chartered train.
第123.106条
§ 123.106
处罚。
Penalties.
(a) 民事处罚。 违反本部分规定的提前到达或离境通知或提前旅客信息要求,除依照其他法律规定适用的其他处罚外,可能依照经修订的1930年关税法第436条(19 U.S.C. 1436)的规定,导致某些民事处罚。此外,与任何此类违规行为相关使用的任何运输工具均应予以扣押和没收。
(a) Civil penalties. Violations of the advance notice of arrival or departure or advance traveler information requirements provided for in this part may result in certain civil penalties, as provided in section 436 of the Tariff Act of 1930, as amended (19 U.S.C. 1436), in addition to other penalties applicable under other provisions of law. Furthermore, any conveyance used in connection with any such violation is subject to seizure and forfeiture.
(b) 刑事处罚。 负责列车的任何人员,故意未能依照本分部的规定以电子方式传输所需的提前到达或离境通知或提前电子旅客信息的,经定罪后,应依照经修订的1930年关税法第436条(19 U.S.C. 1436)的规定,承担额外的刑事处罚。如果该列车载有或被发现曾载有任何禁止进口至美国的商品(船用物料或其他非船舶运输工具的同等物资除外),该人员应依照经修订的1930年关税法第436条(19 U.S.C. 1436)的规定,承担额外的刑事处罚。
(b) Criminal penalties. Any person in charge of a train who intentionally fails to electronically transmit a required advance notice of arrival or departure or advance electronic traveler information, in accordance with this subpart, will be, upon conviction, subject to additional criminal penalties, as prescribed in section 436 of the Tariff Act of 1930, as amended (19 U.S.C. 1436). If the train has, or is discovered to have had, on board any merchandise (other than sea stores or the equivalent for conveyances other than vessels) the importation of which into the United States is prohibited, such individual is subject to additional criminal penalties as prescribed in section 436 of the Tariff Act of 1930, as amended (19 U.S.C. 1436).
(c) 紧急情况。 因紧急情况而未遵守本分部规定的,CBP将考虑该紧急情况的具体情形,以及若非该紧急情况,承运人本应能够完成所需的传输。
(c) Emergencies. In cases of non-compliance with this subpart due to an emergency, CBP will take into consideration the circumstances of the emergency and whether, but for the emergency, the carrier would have been capable of making the required transmissions.
第123.107条
§ 123.107
可分割性。
Severability.
本分部的任何条款,如经具有管辖权的法院 (印刷版第64871页) 裁定对任何人员或情形的适用无效或不可执行,应被解释为在法律允许的范围内继续对该条款赋予最大效力,包括对情形不相似的人员或不同情形的适用,除非该裁定认定本分部该条款在所有情形下均无效且不可执行,在此情况下,该条款应与本分部的其余部分可分割,且不影响本分部的其余部分。
Any provision of this subpart held to be invalid or unenforceable by a court ( printed page 64871) of competent jurisdiction as applied to any person or circumstance shall be construed so as to continue to give the maximum effect to the provision permitted by law, including as applied to persons not similarly situated or to dissimilar circumstances, unless such holding is that the provision of this subpart is invalid and unenforceable in all circumstances, in which event the provision shall be severable from the remainder of this subpart and shall not affect the remainder thereof.
马克韦恩·马林,
Markwayne Mullin,
美国国土安全部(DHS)部长。
Secretary, U.S. Department of Homeland Security.

3. 关税法第431(b)条和第433(d)条(19 U.S.C. 1431(b), 1433(d))对车辆运输施加了要求。“车辆”一词包括“用作或可用作陆地运输工具的任何种类的车辆或其他装置,但不包括航空器。”关税法第401(b)条(19 U.S.C. 1401(b))。因此,关税法第431(b)条和第433(d)条(19 U.S.C. 1431, 1433)适用于铁路运输。
3.  Sections 431(b) and 433(d) of the Tariff Act (19 U.S.C. 1431(b), 1433(d)) impose requirements on vehicle transportation. The term “vehicle” includes “every description of carriage or other contrivance used, or capable of being used, as a means of transportation on land, but does not include aircraft.” Tariff Act sec. 401(b) (19 U.S.C. 1401(b)). Accordingly, sections 431(b) and 433(d) of the Tariff Act (19 U.S.C. 1431, 1433) apply to rail transportation.

23. 一般而言,美国海关与边境保护局在旅客抵达入境口岸时收集其个人生物信息,例如姓名、出生日期、护照号码、居住国和在美国的地址。美国海关与边境保护局通常不收集与旅客火车行程相关的信息,例如预计抵达时间、行程安排、座位号或行李信息。美国海关与边境保护局在检查过程中通常也不收集旅客的电话号码或电子邮件地址。

CBP does not generally collect information relating to the traveler's train trip, such as estimated time of arrival, itinerary, seat number, or baggage information. CBP also does not generally collect traveler's phone numbers or email address during inspection.

31. 2020年3月11日,世界卫生组织(WHO)总干事将COVID-19定性为大流行病。 见 世界卫生组织,《世卫组织总干事在2020年3月11日COVID-19媒体通报会上的开幕讲话》, https://www.who.int/​director-general/​speeches/​detail/​who-director-general-s-opening-remarks-at-the-media-briefing-on-covid-19---11-march-2020 (最后访问日期:2022年1月31日)。2020年3月13日,总统宣布美国境内爆发的COVID-19疫情构成国家紧急状态。第9994号公告,《联邦纪事》第85卷第15337页(2020年3月18日)(宣布关于新型冠状病毒疾病(COVID-19)疫情的国家紧急状态)。该国家紧急状态于2023年4月10日经联合决议终止。《公法》第118-3号,《美国法律大全》第137卷第6页(2023年4月10日)。COVID-19疫情的爆发及其后续的旅行限制和要求在短期内减少了跨越美国边境的列车数量,并可能对长期产生了未知的影响。 见 美国国土安全部(DHS),《情况说明:国土安全部在边境采取的措施以限制冠状病毒的进一步传播》, https://www.dhs.gov/​archive/​news/​2020/​10/​19/​fact-sheet-dhs-measures-border-limit-further-spread-coronavirus. (最后访问日期:2026年2月18日)。因此,使用2014年至2019年的历史增长率和数据来估算列车交通量,并不能反映COVID-19大流行所带来的任何影响。在最初编制本分析时,尚不清楚大流行将对未来列车交通量或列车乘客人数造成何种程度的减少,也不清楚CBP在现有数据条件下将如何精确估算此类影响。因此,CBP在本分析中使用的客运列车和货运列车预测数据,预计在本分析所涉期间将被高估,从而可能导致对本规则的成本和收益的高估。
31.  On March 11, 2020, the World Health Organization (WHO) Director General characterized COVID-19 as a pandemic. See WHO, WHO Director-General's opening remarks at the media briefing on COVID-19—11 March 2020, https://www.who.int/​director-general/​speeches/​detail/​who-director-general-s-opening-remarks-at-the-media-briefing-on-covid-19---11-march-2020 (last accessed Jan. 31, 2022). On March 13, 2020, the President declared that the COVID-19 outbreak in the United States constituted a national emergency. Proclamation No. 9994, 85 FR 15337 (Mar. 18, 2020) (Declaring a National Emergency Concerning the Novel Coronavirus Disease (COVID-19) Outbreak). This national emergency was terminated by joint resolution on April 10, 2023. Public Law 118-3, 137 Stat. 6 (Apr. 10, 2023). The outbreak of COVID-19 and subsequent travel restrictions and requirements reduced the number of trains crossing the U.S. border in the short run and may have had an unknown impact in the long run. See DHS, Fact Sheet: DHS Measures on the Border to Limit the Further Spread of Coronavirus, https://www.dhs.gov/​archive/​news/​2020/​10/​19/​fact-sheet-dhs-measures-border-limit-further-spread-coronavirus. (last accessed Feb. 18, 2026). Consequently, using historical growth rates and figures from 2014-2019 to estimate train traffic does not reflect any impacts from the COVID-19 pandemic. When this analysis was initially prepared, it was not clear what level of reductions the pandemic would have on future train traffic or train passenger numbers or how CBP would estimate such an impact with any precision given the available data. Therefore, passenger train and cargo train projections CBP uses in this analysis are expected to be overestimations for the period of the analysis, resulting in potential overestimations of this rule's costs and benefits.

44. 这与已提交旅客预先信息的其他运输方式相同,例如商业航空器、私人航空器和船舶。例如,对于商业航空器而言,航空承运人通常遵循美国海关与边境保护局(CBP)的建议,但如果其运送的外国人被CBP认定为不可入境,该承运人可能被要求支付将该不可入境外国人运出美国的费用,并可能被处以罚款。 参见,例如, INA第231条(c)款、(d)款(8 U.S.C. 1221(c)、(d));INA第273条(a)款第(1)项、(b)款(8 U.S.C. 1323(a)(1)、(b))。

For example, for commercial aircraft, air carriers typically follow CBP's recommendations but if they transport an alien who CBP determines is inadmissible, the carrier may be required to pay for transporting the inadmissible alien out of the United States and be subject to fines. See, e.g., INA 231(c), (d) (8 U.S.C. 1221(c), (d)); INA 273(a)(1), (b) (8 U.S.C. 1323(a)(1), (b)).
